- a Switch 2 game using DLSS crashes after minutes of play, at a moment it makes a DLSS network again or evaluates one at new sizes (`[nvn] DLSS network …` and a new `DLSS: MetalFX temporal scaler …` line, for example a 1920x972 output), with `*** Terminating app due to uncaught exception 'NSInvalidArgumentException', reason: '-[AGXG17XFamilyMLContext executable]: unrecognized selector sent to instance …'` in `-[_MTL4MachineLearningCommandEncoder encodeToCommandQueue:]` under `rec_commit` / `mtl_sub_commit` (the host exits with 134). The class named varies with what took the freed memory, and many runs pass. → [DLSS scalers released under their encoders](../reference/rendering.md#dlss-scalers-released-under-their-encoders)
- a Switch 2 game's frame in its heavy scenes is dominated by one compute dispatch between the render-resolution passes and the output-resolution ones: Donkey Kong Bananza's upscaler takes 3.5-5 ms of a 24 ms GPU frame on an M1 in handheld mode (960x540 → 1920x1080, `DM_NVN_GPUPROF`), and the game cannot reach its 60 fps interval there. → [A game's own upscaler drawn by MetalFX](../reference/rendering.md#a-games-own-upscaler-drawn-by-metalfx)
- the launcher's **3D upscaler** set to MetalFX changes nothing: the log names the game's upscaler shaders, no `first dispatch of … drawn by MetalFX` line follows, and `DM_NVN_GPUPROF` still shows a compute encoder of four dispatches, about 3 ms on an M2 Pro, between the render-resolution passes and the 1920x1080 ones. → [An upscaler that runs as a chain of dispatches drawn by MetalFX](../reference/rendering.md#an-upscaler-that-runs-as-a-chain-of-dispatches-drawn-by-metalfx)
- none reproduced. Expected: a `kIOGPUCommandBufferCallbackErrorPageFault` in a frame's last command buffers, with `DM_UPSCALER=metalfx` and dynamic resolution, as the resolution steps to a size not seen lately. → [A scaler the cache gave up left the residency set at once](../reference/rendering.md#a-scaler-the-cache-gave-up-left-the-residency-set-at-once)
- with `DM_UPSCALER=metalfx`, a game's picture is washed out wherever it upscales: lifted shadows and low contrast, the scene only, with the UI drawn after it correct; with `DM_UPSCALER=game`, or at a size the game does not upscale, it is right. → [An upscaler's colour read from its code](../reference/rendering.md#an-upscalers-colour-read-from-its-code)
- with `DM_UPSCALER=metalfx` the colours are right but fine texture and edges look harsher than with the game's network: a woven cap's threads, grass blades, the rims of stones. → [MetalFX's picture is crisper than a game's own upscaler](../reference/rendering.md#metalfxs-picture-is-crisper-than-a-games-own-upscaler)
- tall blue / textured panels that flickered frame to frame: the fountain's vertex shader pushed the water surface to the top of the screen. → [Metal 4 does not order operations inside one encoder](../reference/rendering.md#metal-4-does-not-order-operations-inside-one-encoder)
- a GPU-driven cull drops whole groups of objects in some frames, at random, with the camera still: in Ocarina of Time's Hyrule Castle courtyard the flower bushes showed their dark inner body through holes where front leaves and flowers should be, in about 40 % of frames (M1 Max). → [An indirect dispatch read its group counts before the dispatch that wrote them](../reference/rendering.md#an-indirect-dispatch-read-its-group-counts-before-the-dispatch-that-wrote-them)
- MetalFX scales a picture whose drawing has not finished (stale or half-drawn input), or the pass after a scaler reads its output before the scaler wrote it: one frame now and then where the GPU is busy. No game has been seen to show it; the tests below show it in every round, or most. → [An encoder of barriers alone orders nothing](../reference/rendering.md#an-encoder-of-barriers-alone-orders-nothing)
- a buffer copy, inline update or fill completes, but a following copy through another Metal buffer over the same allocation reads stale bytes. → [Buffer copies through aliased Metal addresses](../reference/rendering.md#buffer-copies-through-aliased-metal-addresses)
- many render passes with one draw each and high submit time: Metroid Dread's cutscene frames had about 180 passes with one draw each (about 5 ms of submit time per frame on the Mac). → [Inline uniform updates inside a render pass](../reference/rendering.md#inline-uniform-updates-inside-a-render-pass)
- hundreds of render passes a frame and heavy attachment traffic when a game updates a small part of a large uniform block before each draw group. → [Partial-cover uniform shadows keep passes open](../reference/rendering.md#partial-cover-uniform-shadows-keep-passes-open)
- a scene runs at 20–27 fps where similar ones hold 60, with the GPU time about three times its usual (draw counts unchanged). The `residency set` line shows `passes ended to land them for a read` in the tens of thousands, every one `refused … other GPU writes`, and about three times the usual `load-all store-all passes`. A burst of 1,200 such refusals at each world load is the short form. → [A storage buffer bound with size 0 counted as a write anywhere](../reference/rendering.md#a-storage-buffer-bound-with-size-0-counted-as-a-write-anywhere)
- the `residency set` line shows `passes ended to land them for a read` in the tens of thousands, every one `refused … earlier submission running`. The slower the GPU, the more passes, and each pass makes it slower: a scene stays at a very low frame rate once it falls in. → [Inline updates of a running submission refused uniform shadows](../reference/rendering.md#inline-updates-of-a-running-submission-refused-uniform-shadows)
- a scene that holds 60 fps with the GPU at about 5 ms a frame drops for seconds at a time to 35–40 fps, with the GPU at 20–26 ms, after a slow frame such as a load. The `residency set` line shows tens of thousands of refusals per 300 frames, every one `a report still to land` (before this fix they were counted as `earlier submission running`). → [Counter reports of a running submission refused uniform shadows by page](../reference/rendering.md#counter-reports-of-a-running-submission-refused-uniform-shadows-by-page)
- one scene of a game drops to 1-2 fps and stays there, with the GPU busy 400-600 ms a frame and about ten times the render passes of the scenes before it, many of one draw each on the same target. The `residency set` line shows `passes ended to land them for a read` in the tens of thousands, nearly all `refused … earlier submission running`, with some `unreadable`. A pass dump (`DM_NVN_PASSDUMP`) names `draw_` → `ubd_conflict` as the end of those passes (`E <pass> pass ends in <function>:<line>`). → [A submission past 8,192 queued writes counted as writing everywhere](../reference/rendering.md#a-submission-past-8192-queued-writes-counted-as-writing-everywhere)
- `stage 0 cb5 has no uniform buffer bound` (or another stage and slot, such as `stage 0 cb0`), once per run. → [An unbound uniform slot reads the zero bank](../reference/rendering.md#an-unbound-uniform-slot-reads-the-zero-bank)
- `[RED] stage 5 cb1 has no uniform buffer bound: shader … address 0 size 0 (frame 0)`, once per run; now `stage 5 cb1 is bound empty by the game (address 0, size 0): shader … reads it as 0`, once, not red. → [A uniform slot the game binds empty](../reference/rendering.md#a-uniform-slot-the-game-binds-empty)
- GPU time per frame doubles with many small dispatches (Age of Calamity: GPU 9 → 18 ms a frame on the Mac; the iPhone's 15 → 51 ms, 17 fps). → [Compute dispatches share the copy encoder](../reference/rendering.md#compute-dispatches-share-the-copy-encoder)
- with each dispatch in an encoder of its own (`DM_NVN_DISPATCH_OWN=1` or `DM_NVN_PASSGPU`, both since removed) the host crashed in the Metal driver (`CRASH sig=11 addr=0x98` in `AGX::ComputeContext<…>::prepareForEnqueue(bool)`) at a compute shader's first read through a texture view of another format; by default (dispatches sharing the copy encoder) that read can see the texture's old pixels. Metal validation reports `Command buffer already has an open command encoder` for the first. → [Compute bindings prepare texture copies before the dispatch encoder](../reference/rendering.md#compute-bindings-prepare-texture-copies-before-the-dispatch-encoder)
- dialog boxes with holes the size of their text, and white specks where text faded. → [Fragment-ordering barriers between draws that write images](../reference/rendering.md#fragment-ordering-barriers-between-draws-that-write-images)
- a bright or purple patch grows on a still scene. Presented luma rose from 29 through 66 and 105 to 119, against 41 in a clean run. The corruption stayed in both phases of an in-run A/B (`DM_AB`). → [Draw passes without fragment order read unfinished attachments, and the damage persists](../reference/rendering.md#draw-passes-without-fragment-order-read-unfinished-attachments-and-the-damage-persists)
- a texture copied from a staging buffer shows another texture's blocks (Catherine: Full Body's cutscene subtitle band a hard-edged dark box with a magenta stroke through it). → [A segment committed after a buffer → texture copy](../reference/rendering.md#a-segment-committed-after-a-buffer--texture-copy)
- a scaled texture copy skipped with a red line (Metal blits cannot scale); format-changing, depth-stencil and mirrored copies. → [Copies a blit cannot do, drawn](../reference/rendering.md#copies-a-blit-cannot-do-drawn)
- `[RED] Downsample case not implemented: 320x176 1 samples format 70 → 80x48`; a missing bloom. → [`Downsample` of a single-sample texture](../reference/rendering.md#downsample-of-a-single-sample-texture)
- `[RED] NVN unimplemented function called: nvnCommandBufferTiledDownsample`; menus render but the stage is black or incomplete. → [Tiled multisample resolves](../reference/rendering.md#tiled-multisample-resolves)
- no ambient occlusion (Mac); on a device the result depends on whatever that memory holds. → [Depth-stencil copies carry the stencil](../reference/rendering.md#depth-stencil-copies-carry-the-stencil)
- `DM_NVN_GPUPROF=every:1` prints no per-frame reports and fills its timestamp heap across frames. → [Periodic GPU profiles close at frame boundaries](../reference/rendering.md#periodic-gpu-profiles-close-at-frame-boundaries)
- with `DM_NVN_GPUPROF`, the host aborts minutes in on `-[AGXG16XFamilyComputeContext_mtlnext writeTimestampWithGranularity:afterStage:intoHeap:atIndex:]: unrecognized selector`, `up_flush` on the stack; or a profiled render pass with a wrong GPU time. → [GPU profiles stamp only the encoder they began](../reference/rendering.md#gpu-profiles-stamp-only-the-encoder-they-began)
- none seen in a game. A draw could bind a texture, sampler, storage or uniform buffer from before a binding change: the draw's shader had written its block earlier in the pass, another shader wrote a block on the same stage after the change, and the first shader drew again. PR #2249 found the mechanism by reading (#1898's 56e7ee16). → [An argument block reused after another shader of its stage encoded](../reference/rendering.md#an-argument-block-reused-after-another-shader-of-its-stage-encoded)
- an argument block or a padded uniform binding reads bytes from another arena after a nested allocation rolls over to a new block. Two synthetic GPU tests return incorrect results without their respective fixes. In a game, incorrect argument addresses can also cause GPU address faults. → [An argument block points into another arena after a nested allocation](../reference/rendering.md#an-argument-block-points-into-another-arena-after-a-nested-allocation)
- the 3D scene black (targets cleared again before they are read); `[RED] SetRenderEnableConditional: the host draws anyway`; with a game that skips clears under render conditions, scenes black behind the HUD (Mario vs. Donkey Kong's levels, 1.0.1). → [Conditional rendering evaluated at replay](../reference/rendering.md#conditional-rendering-evaluated-at-replay)
- objects missing that the game draws under `SetRenderEnableConditional`, while a pass dump shows their conditions reading `values 0 / 0 → not drawn` on records no counter report writes. → [A condition on a word the GPU writes](../reference/rendering.md#a-condition-on-a-word-the-gpu-writes)
- GPU time spent on objects the game's occlusion queries culled: a pass dump shows `conditional render … (unknown, drawn)` on query records whose submission completed long before. → [Occlusion conditions read once landed](../reference/rendering.md#occlusion-conditions-read-once-landed)
- a 3D scene black or missing behind the HUD, only in scenes with many occlusion queries; a pass dump shows conditions reading `values 1 / 0` on a record a report of the same submission has just written, and clear-only passes on targets just drawn to. Scenes with fewer queries are fine. → [Report tables that dropped entries](../reference/rendering.md#report-tables-that-dropped-entries)
- random or sustained black 3D frames while the 2D HUD keeps drawing in busy or wide-view scenes, with no `[RED]` lines; `DM_NVN_ENC_THREAD=1` turns flickering into multi-second black screens, and restarting the game can change whether a session hits it. → [Running-write hash collisions and page-set overflow taint conditional renders](../reference/rendering.md#running-write-hash-collisions-and-page-set-overflow-taint-conditional-renders)
- `[RED] visibility slots exhausted`. → [Query slots running out inside a pass](../reference/rendering.md#query-slots-running-out-inside-a-pass)
- GPU time on draws that sit between a samples-passed report and the next reset, in games that wrap single draws in queries of their own; a same-process `DM_NVN_VIS_NEED=ab` run shows it. → [Occlusion counting for draws no report can see](../reference/rendering.md#occlusion-counting-for-draws-no-report-can-see)
- characters a few metres away drawn as their hats and props only, their bodies missing from the main view while their shadows are there; near characters draw fully. A log line or a probe shows every samples-passed report reading 0. → [Samples-passed reports with no reset before them](../reference/rendering.md#samples-passed-reports-with-no-reset-before-them)

## Shaders

- the shader split stops on one archive with a Python traceback (an `IndexError` or `struct.error` naming no file), or a reader's `[RED]` line about a format it does not know, and nothing is written; the NSP script loses the build at the split. → [A file no reader reads](../reference/shaders.md#a-file-no-reader-reads)
- the split leaves out archives whose members do not overlap: `[RED] <file>/<member>.szs/#7: the archive members add up to more than 16 times the file (members that overlap)`, then `[RED] <n> file(s) could not be read`, and a registered profile's build stops there. → [A Yaz0 member that expands is not overlap](../reference/shaders.md#a-yaz0-member-that-expands-is-not-overlap)
- the shader build fails one agl or terrain shader no run ever asks for, with `Ampere instruction 481 at 0x0 (000000000000000000000000020c0481) is not implemented` (a vertex shader's SPH read as an instruction); in the map, a shader whose control stage disagrees with its code (a compute control with a vertex SPH, a pixel control with a vertex SPH) and whose stored control section is longer than the file's real ones (0x200, 0x1100, 0x1400 against 0xe0). Also: no `pso_pairs.tsv` rows from a file whose lowest such entry hides its binary array. → [A control section's words read as a BAHS entry](../reference/shaders.md#a-control-sections-words-read-as-a-bahs-entry)
- `shader_split.py` finds 0 shaders in a RomFS of zstd files named `.zst`. → [Zstd files recognised by their magic](../reference/shaders.md#zstd-files-recognised-by-their-magic)
- `[RED] shader <key> is not in the shader map` for shaders whose code is in a `.bfsha` the split reads, `[RED] NVN unimplemented function called: nvnCommandBufferDrawMeshTasksIndirect`, and grass and ferns missing. → [Task and mesh shaders in BNSH, and indirect mesh draws](../reference/shaders.md#task-and-mesh-shaders-in-bnsh-and-indirect-mesh-draws)
- a Switch 2 game's picture is far too bright and warm wherever the view opens out: lifted blacks, yellow skin, a pale veil. The build log has `Invalid reduction type: F32FtzRn`, easy to miss among thousands of lines. → [Float atomics in storage memory](../reference/shaders.md#float-atomics-in-storage-memory)
- a shader that reads a float-add loop's retry flag after the loop has an undeclared identifier after the native-atomic rewrite. → [A float atomic add's retry flag survives the rewrite](../reference/shaders.md#a-float-atomic-adds-retry-flag-survives-the-rewrite)
- on an M5 or M5 Pro Mac on macOS 26.6.2, `[RED] failed to create compute pipeline for <key>_b: Compilation failed due to an interrupted connection: XPC_ERROR_CONNECTION_INTERRUPTED` and `[RED] pipeline archive: the compiler service died under a compile` at every start, with `MTLCompilerService` reports ending in `LLVM ERROR: cannot select: … intrinsic(@llvm.agx3.disable.smashing.float)` in `agc.main.constant_program`; the dispatches the compiler did not give leave flat light-blue areas in Kokiri Forest. → [Uniform float atomic adds crash Apple's AGX3 compiler](../reference/shaders.md#uniform-float-atomic-adds-crash-apples-agx3-compiler)
- dotted horizontal rows in blocks of the picture, in smooth and blurred areas, from a Switch 2 game's upscaler: the frame before it is clean and the frame after it is not. The same input gives a different picture each run. Bright scenes show more of them. → [textureGatherOffsets and a loop over its offsets](../reference/shaders.md#texturegatheroffsets-and-a-loop-over-its-offsets)
- when an AV1 movie starts, `[RED] shader <key> is not in the shader map` for 11 compute shaders and `[RED] the dispatched program has no compute shader (or it is not mapped)`. → [`nn::ndve`'s movie enhancement file](../reference/shaders.md#nnndves-movie-enhancement-file)
- `draw skipped: no render pipeline (vertex - fragment -)` in a game's final passes; shaders "not in the shader map", all from `agl_ndsr.pck`. A short synthetic pack found by fuzzing raised `struct.error: iterative unpacking requires a buffer of a multiple of 16 bytes` while choosing the record start. → [The NDSR pack's record start](../reference/shaders.md#the-ndsr-packs-record-start)
- the shader split stops with `[RED] <file> BNSH @0x… variation <n>: a geometry shader: this chain translates only the vertex, pixel, compute, task and mesh shaders of an NVN2 game, as Ocarina of Time has no other` (also `a hull shader`, `a domain shader`, `a ray tracing shader`, and `<file>#BAHS@0x…`, `#NDSR@`, `#NDVE@` in place of the BNSH variation), `[RED] <file> BNSH @0x… variation <n> <stage>: not an NVN2 control section (version 0xc9): an NVN1 (Maxwell) shader, which this chain does not translate` or `[RED] <file>#BAHS@0x…: an NVN1 (Maxwell) shader entry: this chain translates only NVN2 (Switch 2) shaders`, and writes no map. The shader build stops before its first job with `[RED] <map>: shader <key> is a <stage> shader in package <package>: this chain translates only the vertex, pixel, compute, task and mesh shaders of an NVN2 game (each in the package of its stage), as Ocarina of Time has no other`, `[RED] <package>: shader <key> has no NVN2 control section (magic 0x98761234, version 0xc9): this chain translates only NVN2 (Switch 2, Ampere) shaders, and an NVN1 (Maxwell) record is not skipped but stops the build`, `[RED] <package>: shader <key> is an NVN2 ray tracing module (control stage 8): this chain has no ray tracing path, as the game has no such shader` or `[RED] <package>: shader <key> is not a <stage> shader (control stage byte <n>, SPH shader type <n>): this chain translates only vertex, pixel, compute, task and mesh shaders, each from the package of its stage`. A vertex shader that writes the layer fails its own build, `[layer output]` in the summary: `the vertex shader writes the layer (render_target_array_index), which this chain and the host have no path for` (the build goes on; the packing script stops at it as a shader regression when the previous build translated it). At run time: `[RED] a shader's control section is not NVN2's (magic 0x98761234, version 0xc9): no shader key for it, as the shader library holds only NVN2 shaders`, then `FATAL program stage <n> has no shader key` when the program is first used. → [A shader the chain does not translate stops the split and the build](../reference/shaders.md#a-shader-the-chain-does-not-translate-stops-the-split-and-the-build)
- the offline shader build raises `IndexError: index out of range` in `job_for` while reading `rec[ci + 0x56]`, after the shader split succeeds. → [Optional stage byte in short NVN2 control sections](../reference/shaders.md#optional-stage-byte-in-short-nvn2-control-sections)
- shaders reported missing at random, and their dumped control sections are garbage (Unity). Also: about 130 `[RED] shader … is not in the shader map` keys a boot, nearly all different from run to run, all single-stage programs, with `program has no vertex shader`, and every frame the same picture of grey noise; with `DM_NVN_SHDUMP` set the game draws correctly. → [Program keys computed inside `nvnProgramSetShaders`](../reference/shaders.md#program-keys-computed-inside-nvnprogramsetshaders)
- with shader job `debug = false`, SPIR-V generation throws `GetLocalPointer` / `KeyNotFoundException` or casts an `AstOperation` to an `AstOperand`. Other inputs compile but read a call's output before calling it, lose an unused-return call's side effects, or read local memory after an intervening store instead of preserving its earlier value. → [Optimized shader calls and memory snapshots](../reference/shaders.md#optimized-shader-calls-and-memory-snapshots)
- Metal warns that a boolean is used uninitialized on a path through a translated shader. A generated `if (!bypass) predicate = ...; if (predicate || bypass)` reads `predicate` before testing the bypass flag; the analogous AND form has the same defect. → [A jump enters a condition whose definition it bypassed](../reference/shaders.md#a-jump-enters-a-condition-whose-definition-it-bypassed)
- a shader build accepts tools that differ from `dmsh_chain.lock`; after an override build, returning to the tested Metal compiler can still restore the override's libraries. → [Tested tools and compiler-specific shader caches](../reference/shaders.md#tested-tools-and-compiler-specific-shader-caches)
- after an Xcode update the build stops with `[RED] this Mac's Metal compiler is not the one the shaders were tested with (dm/gfx/tools/dmsh_chain.lock): metal_macosx Apple metal version 32023.922 …`; `xcodebuild -downloadComponent MetalToolchain`, as the message said, downloads the same untested compiler again. → [Metal compiler of a newer Xcode](../reference/shaders.md#metal-compiler-of-a-newer-xcode)
- `regress.py` waits for other jobs to end while the only reported jobs are sleeping `MSBuild.dll /nodemode:1 /nodeReuse:true` workers using 0% CPU. → [Idle MSBuild workers block regression measurements](../reference/shaders.md#idle-msbuild-workers-block-regression-measurements)
- SPIRV-Cross can't parse the SPIR-V (`nullptr` exception). → [SPIRV-Cross parse failures](../reference/shaders.md#spirv-cross-parse-failures)
- `Fragment input(s) user(locnN) mismatching vertex shader output type(s) or not written by vertex shader` and the draw is skipped; the host reports `[RED] failed to create render pipeline (vertex … fragment …)`. Offline packing fails with `pso_offline: metal-tt failed` for the same reason. → [Pixel shader inputs the vertex shader does not write](../reference/shaders.md#pixel-shader-inputs-the-vertex-shader-does-not-write)
- `[RED] task / mesh pipeline: task - mesh … fragment …: … is not found in the output from the previous stage`, and the mesh draws of that program are skipped. Ocarina of Time's foliage: the point meshes `3a6340a04bf09797` and `a203347bb7bdbd03` write only a point size and a position, while their pixel shaders (`2a7c48d595feaaeb`, `cd609edc78b467d8`) read generic attribute 5 (pull request #1590). → [Pixel shader inputs the mesh shader does not write](../reference/shaders.md#pixel-shader-inputs-the-mesh-shader-does-not-write)
- `[RED] task / mesh pipeline: … Total mesh size (35840) exceeds the maximum mesh size allowed (32768)`, and the program's mesh draws are skipped: Ocarina of Time's foliage mesh `626fbf48041cb82b` with pixel shader `c5eb985e769e317f` (`Foliage.Product.110`, `#BNSH@0x1b1000#var3`), once padded. → [Mesh outputs as wide as their pixel shaders read](../reference/shaders.md#mesh-outputs-as-wide-as-their-pixel-shaders-read)
- outdoors dim and nothing casts a sun shadow (every shadow-map lookup fails). → [Unwritten components of a written output start as (0, 0, 0, 1)](../reference/shaders.md#unwritten-components-of-a-written-output-start-as-0-0-0-1)
- rows of dark dots along contour lines of alpha-tested surfaces drawn after a depth pre-pass (Tears of the Kingdom: the stone lattice in front of the Great Sky Island start, as reported by MacNativeGames ae8f4f4; not reproduced here). On Switch 2, GPU time: every covered pixel of a shader that writes memory was shaded (PRAGMATA's cabin on an M4 Pro: GPU 19.0 ms a frame, p95 frame 64 ms, a quarter of the frames over 33 ms). → [Early fragment tests from the control section](../reference/shaders.md#early-fragment-tests-from-the-control-section)
- a simulation or filter that reads neighbouring texels misbehaves though every constant is right. In the translated MSL a variable is declared with no value and read at once (`int _139; int _141 = _139;`), used as a coordinate. On Ocarina of Time: grass whose blades shift at 10 Hz, with a 6-frame cycle in the depth buffer and a wind velocity texture of strong stripes. → [Dead-code removal dropped a coordinate of a multi-output instruction](../reference/shaders.md#dead-code-removal-dropped-a-coordinate-of-a-multi-output-instruction)
- the ground kept stale 32×32 blocks where the G-buffer draw failed the depth test: blocks of a stale image where a depth pre-pass and a later pass with other vertex shaders meet (Odyssey: 32×32 blocks on every fourth frame). With mesh shaders: white shapes on plants for single frames, at other places each frame while the leaves move (Ocarina of Time). → [Position invariance across shaders](../reference/shaders.md#position-invariance-across-shaders)
- white clouds over the ground, coming and going (Ocarina of Time: over the ground mist between Kokiri Forest and Hyrule Field, and over the ferns at the camera in Kokiri Forest while they sway). The scene target holds NaN pixels after a fog or lighting pass and bloom spreads each into a white blot; dropping that pass removes them. → [A NaN colour under a zero blend weight](../reference/shaders.md#a-nan-colour-under-a-zero-blend-weight)
- pixel shader compilation fails with `reference type must have explicit address space qualifier` at `dm_blend0(main0_out& dm_o, ...)`: every iOS build, and a Mac build made on macOS 26. A Mac build made on macOS 27 compiled, so the fault passed where it was written. → [The blend helper's output reference on iOS](../reference/shaders.md#the-blend-helpers-output-reference-on-ios)
- three translator tests (`test_dmsh_gather_offsets.py`, `test_dmsh_wrapv.py`, `test_dmsh_raw_last.py`) failed with a `NameError` or a non-literal value once the Mac compile named its deployment target, and three others compiled with flags the build does not use: `test_dmsh_tg_fence.py` without `-fwrapv` and the deployment target, `test_image_atomic_bounds.py` without `-O1`, `-fwrapv` and the deployment target, `test_shader_pch.py` with a hand copy that would not follow a change. → [Tests compiled with copies of the Mac compile](../reference/shaders.md#tests-compiled-with-copies-of-the-mac-compile)
- dark specks flickering in a ground mist, a few pixels each, at different places every frame (Ocarina of Time in Kokiri Forest; the console shows none). Dropping the mist removes them, as does dropping its depth-only pass. → [gl_FragCoord.z against the depth stored for its own surface](../reference/shaders.md#gl_fragcoordz-against-the-depth-stored-for-its-own-surface)
- making a pipeline archive prints `Max Unstruct ID 758 in function agc.main in shader 13941 exceeds supported limit`, and one pipeline takes many minutes, in `metal-tt` offline or in the host's prebuild on the device (`Preparing pipelines` holding one count for minutes). The line is a note, not an error: `metal-tt` goes on, exits 0 and writes that pipeline into the archive. → [Front end at -O1: Apple's control-flow lowering](../reference/shaders.md#front-end-at--o1-apples-control-flow-lowering)
- the Mac app's (or the iPhone build's) pipeline archive step stops in its first group with `[RED] pso_offline: metal-tt failed for applegpu_<arch> (exit 1): applegpu-nt: error: cannot find private metadata at offset '8' in '…/<key>_bp.metallib'`, and the app is packed without an archive, compute pipelines included. The host's prebuild then makes every base on the first start. Only a game with the near = 0 depth mode (`depth_mode zero`) whose vertex shaders declare the border corrections' constant. → [Specialized second functions in the offline pipeline archive](../reference/shaders.md#specialized-second-functions-in-the-offline-pipeline-archive)
- the Mac app's (or the iPhone build's) pipeline archive step runs for many hours: a group of 1,000 bases runs past its time limit, and `compile_split` leaves out single pipelines that each ran past theirs (`[RED] pso_offline: applegpu_<gpu>: left out of the archive, the device builds it`), while `air-nt` grows past the Mac's memory and swap fills. Alone, such a pipeline does not finish in 10 minutes and takes 10 to 13 GB, where the build before took seconds. On the device, the same pipelines would hold `Preparing pipelines` for as long. → [Tests of one uniform word folded into switches](../reference/shaders.md#tests-of-one-uniform-word-folded-into-switches)
- the shader build prints `fatal error: bracket nesting level exceeded maximum of 256` and `<key>.metal: does not compile`, and the shader is left out. → [Bracket nesting past the front end's 256](../reference/shaders.md#bracket-nesting-past-the-front-ends-256)
- the half of Mario facing the camera's left was white (GitHub issue 72, on an iPhone). → [min, max and clamp with a NaN operand](../reference/shaders.md#min-max-and-clamp-with-a-nan-operand)
- a pipeline red line for a float output meeting an integer attachment (the red line prints the row to add); a BC encoder's pipeline fails. → [Integer fragment outputs declared by hand](../reference/shaders.md#integer-fragment-outputs-declared-by-hand)
- a pixel shader that writes depth and only some components of a colour target writes the wrong depth: in the translated MSL, `gl_FragDepth` takes a value the shader computes a step before its last one, or a colour. → [A pixel shader's depth after a partly written target](../reference/shaders.md#a-pixel-shaders-depth-after-a-partly-written-target)
- `failed to create render pipeline … output of type float4 is not compatible with a MTLPixelFormatR32Uint color attachment` (or `MTLPixelFormatRGBA16Uint`); every draw into the target skipped. → [Integer entry points for integer colour outputs](../reference/shaders.md#integer-entry-points-for-integer-colour-outputs)
- a deferred scene with part of its materials unlit or black: grass, ground and trees dark while wood and characters look right, whole areas of a map black. There may be no red line. A texture dump shows an integer G-buffer target (`fmt=73`, RGBA8Uint) holding only zeros, and the lighting pass reads it. Or `[RED] shader … writes colour target …, an integer format, as a float`. → [Integer outputs told by the pipeline's targets](../reference/shaders.md#integer-outputs-told-by-the-pipelines-targets)
- an Unreal landscape drawn from a runtime virtual texture is one flat lit colour on the Mac (green grass with no texture) and a grid of repeating tiles on an iPhone; rocks, characters and everything else right; no red line. A texture dump shows the virtual texture's BC tile caches full and varied, and its page table (R16Uint, `fmt=23`, 512x512 with 10 mips, the texture the landscape's pixel shader reads at `reg130`) holding 255 in every entry: level 15, page address 15, so the sample lands on one texel of the cache. Since this entry, `[RED] shader … writes colour target …, an integer format, as a float, and has no integer entry point`. → [Integer entry points for flat inputs passed through beside constants](../reference/shaders.md#integer-entry-points-for-flat-inputs-passed-through-beside-constants)
- on the `MTLDevice` path (the iPhone's default) `[RED] failed to create render pipeline … output of type float4 is not compatible with a MTLPixelFormatR8Uint color attachment` for many fragment shaders, and most of the scene missing (black sky, no characters). With `DM_PSO_MTL4=1` the same pipelines build from Metal 4 bases and the picture looks right, but `[RED] shader … writes colour target 5, an integer format, as a float, and has no integer entry point` stays: the target takes undefined values. A signed integer target (`R32Sint`, …) is refused the same way, by every shader. → [Typed outputs for every pixel shader](../reference/shaders.md#typed-outputs-for-every-pixel-shader)
- outdoors the whole screen blurred (depth of field out of focus everywhere). → [Depth mode near = 0 needs the per-mode entry points](../reference/shaders.md#depth-mode-near--0-needs-the-per-mode-entry-points)
- `SPIRV-Cross threw an exception: Subgroup builtins are not available in this type of function`. → [Subgroup operations rewritten for a one-lane subgroup](../reference/shaders.md#subgroup-operations-rewritten-for-a-one-lane-subgroup)
- `[RED] NVN unimplemented function called: nvnCommandBufferDrawMeshTasks`, and a build that fails with `Ampere instruction 927 ... is not implemented` in shaders the map calls vertex or domain; what they draw is missing. → [NVN2 task and mesh shaders](../reference/shaders.md#nvn2-task-and-mesh-shaders)
- large flat panels in front of the sky, the clouds behind them blinking at times, in some places and not others. In a `DM_NVN_PASSDUMP_ALL` dump every task and mesh shader's storage buffer shows as `st3` / `st4 sbufp reg6` or `reg7` `dummy addr=0 size=0`, though the game binds buffers for those stages. → [Task and mesh draws reading the dummy buffer](../reference/shaders.md#task-and-mesh-draws-reading-the-dummy-buffer)
- a game with task and mesh shaders runs at a lower internal resolution than it could, or below its frame rate, and the GPU time sits in the pass that draws its mesh work (a render pass's start stamp is written after its vertex stage, where Metal runs the object and mesh stages, so in a `DM_NVN_GPUPROF` profile the mesh work shows as the gap before that pass or as its length). `DM_NVN_SKIP_MESH=1` (diagnostic: every task and mesh draw dropped, the picture wrong) takes most of that time away. → [Stage memory byte stores through a compare-and-swap loop](../reference/shaders.md#stage-memory-byte-stores-through-a-compare-and-swap-loop)
- a mesh shader whose stage array is 6-16 KB runs slower than its instruction count suggests, and the foliage mesh pass of Ocarina of Time takes a large share of a Kokiri Forest frame on an M3 Pro. Shrinking the array (timing only) made the same code faster. → [Mesh stage memory of 6 KB and more in a device slice](../reference/shaders.md#mesh-stage-memory-of-6-kb-and-more-in-a-device-slice)
- white blotches over Kokiri Forest's foliage, walkway and ground in Ocarina of Time, which are the house light tiles. The light-tile pass of task `a5288d0a6c4971fd` with meshes `e8c667282ba7bd9b` and `d4171cf9adf0504b` writes constant white through fragment `ea1bf96b72e72476`, and its triangles were corrupt. → [Mesh stage memory reserves the primitive index bytes for every primitive block](../reference/shaders.md#mesh-stage-memory-reserves-the-primitive-index-bytes-for-every-primitive-block)
- a mesh shader's per-primitive outputs read as other values once it has more than 32 vertices: Ocarina of Time's Poe Sisters and Phantom Ganon paintings (mesh shaders `ee4ef51a4fddc28e` and `83c0dbc51c5481e3`, 128 vertices, 64 primitives, a per-primitive colour) came out solid green or magenta (pull request #1590). A mesh shader that draws points with a point size takes the size as its position's x. → [Mesh attributes: every vertex block before the primitive blocks](../reference/shaders.md#mesh-attributes-every-vertex-block-before-the-primitive-blocks)
- a mesh-shaded draw shows nothing, though its workgroups run: every workgroup ends with a primitive count of 0. Once that is mended, a visibility buffer written from `[[primitive_id]]` holds only the index within the meshlet, and each pixel is shaded from the wrong cluster. → [Mesh shader per-primitive system values: the primitive count overwritten, the primitive id lost](../reference/shaders.md#mesh-shader-per-primitive-system-values-the-primitive-count-overwritten-the-primitive-id-lost)
- thin streaks or stray triangles from a mesh shader over parts of the picture its geometry does not cover (PRAGMATA: white vertical streaks over the cockpit window in the opening). → [Mesh shaders that also address their vertices as records](../reference/shaders.md#mesh-shaders-that-also-address-their-vertices-as-records)
- `NotSupportedException: Ampere 923 …: ISBERD mode f8 in a task or mesh shader` in the shader build; those mesh shaders are left out and their draws skipped (`[RED] DrawMeshTasks without its task and mesh shaders`). → [Mesh shaders that read their own outputs back](../reference/shaders.md#mesh-shaders-that-read-their-own-outputs-back)
- task and mesh shaders fail translation with `ISBERD mode 34 in a task or mesh shader` or `stage output store mode b4 outside a task or mesh shader`; the host cannot load their libraries when the race uses them. → [Subword task and mesh payload accesses](../reference/shaders.md#subword-task-and-mesh-payload-accesses)
- a shader library builds but cannot load: `reflection conversion failed: B binding cp_t2d_cb196608_10568000 has no resolved slot number`, followed by `the dispatched program has no compute shader`. The first race's sky is black. → [Indexed texture handles in NVN2 compute c0](../reference/shaders.md#indexed-texture-handles-in-nvn2-compute-c0)
- a small scan or sort in a compute shader gives wrong results every frame, with no barrier in its loop (Orbitals: Niagara's sorted particle indices); or a compute shader's output differs from one run to the next on the same input, in a few pixels of each tile (agl's NDSR upscaler network in Ocarina of Time: about 0.1 % of its 2560x1440 pixels). → [Threadgroup stores in warp-synchronous code](../reference/shaders.md#threadgroup-stores-in-warp-synchronous-code)
- `[RED] task / mesh pipeline prewarm failed: task … mesh … fragment …: … XPC_ERROR_CONNECTION_INTERRUPTED` once per run, at the prewarm before the first frame; `MTLCompilerService` crashes with `EXC_BAD_ACCESS` in `llvm::MachineOperand::setReg` under `AGCModule<AGX::Impl::MeshProgramKey>::compileLLVMObject`. Runs with the fences also hung on the GPU more often (7 of 9 long runs against 0 of 3), which is suggestive, not proven. → [A fence after every shared memory store crashes Apple's compiler](../reference/shaders.md#a-fence-after-every-shared-memory-store-crashes-apples-compiler)
- a Switch 2 game's shaders do not translate (the translator reads Maxwell: 64-bit instructions behind scheduling words, a 0x50-byte SPH), or, rewritten as Maxwell code, UI text draws as solid boxes or not at all. → [Ampere shader code (Switch 2)](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-shader-code-switch-2)
- a lit pass far slower on an M1 than the M4 Pro's figures suggest, the time spread over every material that loops over the scene's lights; dropping one material's draws (`DM_NVN_UB`) saved only its share. → [Storage words read one by one](../reference/shaders.md#storage-words-read-one-by-one)
- fragment shaders spend GPU time on device loads from storage buffers whose addresses are uniform across the draw. → [Device loads from read-only storage buffers](../reference/shaders.md#device-loads-from-read-only-storage-buffers)
- `reflection conversion failed: B binding cp_i_cb3_0_ has no resolved slot number` at load, then `the dispatched program has no compute shader`; in the build, `SUATOM handle in bank 3` or `SURED handle in bank 3`, and bindings of an unrecognized kind for a typed buffer. Compute passes are skipped. → [Images and buffer textures with their handle in a uniform buffer](../reference/shaders.md#images-and-buffer-textures-with-their-handle-in-a-uniform-buffer)
- a scene lit black with correct geometry. In `DM_NVN_PASSDUMP`, the lighting shader's bindings show `tex … tid=0 dummy` where the G-buffer should be, and a sampler bound where a texture is expected. → [Texture and sampler words in either order](../reference/shaders.md#texture-and-sampler-words-in-either-order)
- `Failed to find handle source for bindless access of type "textureBuffer".` in compute shaders; the build reports deleted reads whose results become zero. → [Compute texture handles loaded through uniform addresses](../reference/shaders.md#compute-texture-handles-loaded-through-uniform-addresses)
- objects from a compute-built list flicker: each is in one of two states, on and off irregularly and independently of the others, while the draws are the same frame to frame and the camera is still. Whole groups may vanish or appear in one frame and stay that way until the list's next rebuild. Shots of consecutive frames show the change per frame jumping between two levels. → [A store only a dispatch's first invocation makes](../reference/shaders.md#a-store-only-a-dispatchs-first-invocation-makes)
- `metal` runs for hours on a shader of about 100 MB of MSL, mostly indentation, stuck in LICM (`hoistRegion` → `ScalarEvolution::forgetValue`), then the GPU compiler's connection breaks; even `-O0` and finished libraries crash it. Hundreds of forced one-pass loops nest around the program. → [Goto elimination forcing loops around a whole program](../reference/shaders.md#goto-elimination-forcing-loops-around-a-whole-program)
- a warp-aggregated atomic in a loop lands for one leader only: PRAGMATA's compute shader `607ceeefb9478700` elects a leader, the lanes with its key leave the loop and reduce with one `SURED.MIN`, and the others go round again; translated, the lanes that left in different iterations voted together, the ballot mixed keys, and the other keys' minimums were lost. Found by an offline differential check of the shader against the instruction stream; no picture traced to it yet. → [A loop exit's subgroup block run by the lanes of every iteration together](../reference/shaders.md#a-loop-exits-subgroup-block-run-by-the-lanes-of-every-iteration-together)
- none met in a game yet: found by differential testing (single instructions with nvdisasm-checked encodings, run on an Apple GPU and compared with an interpreter on Mesa NAK's hardware-checked folds). A 64-bit three-operand add off by 2^32, `POPC R, 0x800000ff` giving 23, a predicate pair from `PLOP3` that reads its own first result, a negative 32-bit index added to a 64-bit address as if positive. → [Ampere carries, PLOP3's second result, POPC / FLO immediates, LEA.SX32 and LEA's negated B](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-carries-plop3s-second-result-popc--flo-immediates-leasx32-and-leas-negated-b)
- a predicate pair that disagrees with itself (`FSETP.LT.AND P0, P1, R2, R3, P0`: P1 combined with the new P0, not the old); a multiply-add off by `2 * a * b` (IMAD read as `a * -b + c`). → [FSETP / ISETP pairs on their own accumulator, and IMAD's B](../reference/shaders.md#fsetp--isetp-pairs-on-their-own-accumulator-and-imads-b)
- `BRX with no target`, or code about 20 % larger than it should be. → [Ampere jump tables read past their end](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-jump-tables-read-past-their-end)
- `BRX with no target found in the c1 jump table`, although the shader's `LDC` of the table's base is on the only path to the BRX. → [An Ampere jump table loaded in a block split later](../reference/shaders.md#an-ampere-jump-table-loaded-in-a-block-split-later)
- `Ampere instruction 308` refused; a neural upscaler's programs missing, so the frame never reaches the window (a black 3D scene). → [MUFU.F16](../reference/shaders.md#mufuf16)
- part of the picture without ambient occlusion, bounded by an arc whose centre is off the screen (the top-left corner), the rest correct; on Donkey Kong Bananza a wedge in the lower right of the SSAO's half-size target when dynamic resolution is at its top (1152x648 for a 2304x1296 scene, docked), a fifth of the picture with the effects at full size (1676x943). → [MUFU.SIN and MUFU.COS of a large number of turns](../reference/shaders.md#mufusin-and-mufucos-of-a-large-number-of-turns)
- a compute shader's results in shared memory land in the wrong elements, or only part of its memory is cleared. An exposure histogram came back with its bins at about four times their index, the scene several stops too dark. → [Scaled shared memory addresses](../reference/shaders.md#scaled-shared-memory-addresses)
- `[RED] failed to create compute pipeline: Threadgroup memory size (49152) exceeds the maximum threadgroup memory allowed (32768)`, thousands a run; the dispatch is skipped. Once moved to device memory: with more than 512 workgroups in a dispatch, or an indirect one, workgroups found other workgroups' data in their shared memory (no game seen doing so). → [Compute shared memory past 32 KB](../reference/shaders.md#compute-shared-memory-past-32-kb)
- on an M1 or M2 only, `[RED] compute pipeline for <key> allows 384 threads a threadgroup, its local size 8x8x8 needs 512 (its dispatches are skipped)`; before the host checked, the pass wrote nothing with no red line and no GPU error: its output kept its old contents (zeros for a volume the pass alone fills). Ocarina of Time loses Kokiri Forest's and Hyrule Field's haze and light shafts (the scene's upper half about 3.7 times darker); the same library is right on an M3 or M4, and the same MSL is right at `-O0`. → [Compute dispatches past the pipeline's thread limit](../reference/shaders.md#compute-dispatches-past-the-pipelines-thread-limit)
- `[RED] shader <key>_b reflection conversion failed: fetch call 0: frag_pos uniform reference out of range`; the shader does not load and its draws are skipped. → [Uniform references past the first kilobyte](../reference/shaders.md#uniform-references-past-the-first-kilobyte)
- the GPU hangs (`kIOGPUCommandBufferCallbackErrorHang`) from the first 3D scene on, or frames of about 88 ms, in a shader with a half-float loop. → [HADD2 and HMUL2 with an immediate](../reference/shaders.md#hadd2-and-hmul2-with-an-immediate)
- wrong shading where a shader needs a cross product or a 2D rotation of half pairs; on Eden-NS2, which first read the mode as a plain negation of the high lane, sun and cloud shadows were lost. → [HFMA2 .H0_NH1](../reference/shaders.md#hfma2-h0_nh1)
- a global load, store or atomic at the wrong address in code that uses `desc[URb][Ra.64]` (the descriptor's value added to the address); a shader with `ATOMG.CAS` refused (`Ampere instruction 3a9 ... is not implemented`); an `ATOMG.INC` / `ATOMG.DEC` / `RED.INC` / `RED.DEC` with no effect. → [Global memory: descriptors, ATOMG.CAS, INC / DEC](../reference/shaders.md#global-memory-descriptors-atomgcas-inc--dec)
- the shader build counts `Shader instruction AtomCas is not implemented.` among the translator's messages, and the shader still loads: its global compare-and-swap does nothing and its result register keeps its old contents (a hash-table insertion or a lock-free update that never lands). → [Maxwell ATOM.CAS](../reference/shaders.md#maxwell-atomcas)
- a load through a pointer the shader read from memory gives 0, and a store or atomic through one does nothing, when the instruction has `.E` and a negative displacement (`LDG.E R4, [R2+-0x4]`); a `RED` with a negative displacement does nothing on either route. → [A negative displacement on a 40-bit global address](../reference/shaders.md#a-negative-displacement-on-a-40-bit-global-address)
- a shared-memory counter that never moves (`ATOMS.INC`, `ATOMS.DEC`: CUDA's atomicInc / atomicDec, the old value read as 0); a lock-free update on shared memory that loops or takes a wrong branch after `ATOMS.CAS` (its result 0 or 1 instead of the old value). → [ATOMS.INC / ATOMS.DEC, and ATOMS.CAS's result](../reference/shaders.md#atomsinc--atomsdec-and-atomscass-result)
- `[translation failed] NotSupportedException: Ampere 307 at …: FRND of a float that is not 32-bit`. → [FRND of a half (FRND.F16)](../reference/shaders.md#frnd-of-a-half-frndf16)
- `[translation failed] NotSupportedException: Ampere instruction f66 … is not implemented`, `Ampere instruction 595 … is not implemented`, or `Ampere 906 …: I2F byte select of a 32-bit source`. → [Ampere forms met on PRAGMATA: TLD through a uniform register, I2F of an immediate, SUATOM.CAS](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-forms-met-on-pragmata-tld-through-a-uniform-register-i2f-of-an-immediate-suatomcas)
- `[translation failed] NotSupportedException: Ampere 326 at …: IPA at an offset`; before the offset is reached, the build's log has `Attribute offset 0x3B4 is not valid` and the read gives 0. → [IPA at an offset, and the barycentric coordinates](../reference/shaders.md#ipa-at-an-offset-and-the-barycentric-coordinates)
- `[translation failed]` for Ampere opcode `3ad` in thousands of pixel shaders (Resident Evil 4: 7,246 of its 42,313 shaders did not build). → [LDTRAM: an attribute at each vertex of the triangle](../reference/shaders.md#ldtram-an-attribute-at-each-vertex-of-the-triangle)
- a shared-memory atomic built from a compare-and-swap loop (INC, DEC, the 64-bit and float operations) spins longer than it should or stores a value computed from stale data when other threads write the word. → [The shared compare-and-swap loop retries with the value it got](../reference/shaders.md#the-shared-compare-and-swap-loop-retries-with-the-value-it-got)
- wrong values in the high lane of half-pair math that scales by one half of a uniform (`HMUL2 R37, R37.H0_H0, c[0x0] [0x168].H0_H0`), or a garbage operand where a half-pair operation reads a 32-bit float (`R20.F32`). → [Half-pair swizzle on a constant, and .F32](../reference/shaders.md#half-pair-swizzle-on-a-constant-and-f32)
- a half comparison with `.FTZ` on a subnormal operand passes where NVIDIA's fails (or the reverse): `HSET2.BF.GT.FTZ R6, R19, RZ` gives 1.0 for a positive subnormal half, 0 on NVIDIA. → [HSETP2 / HSET2 .FTZ](../reference/shaders.md#hsetp2--hset2-ftz)
- integer division results one off for some operands (the compiler's 32-bit division takes its reciprocal from `I2F.U32.RP`), or a half one ulp off (an overflow giving infinity instead of the largest finite value) after `F2F.F16.F32.RM` / `.RP` / `.RZ`. → [I2F and F2F directed rounding](../reference/shaders.md#i2f-and-f2f-directed-rounding)
- `I2F` from a signed or unsigned 64-bit register pair to f32 can give the wrong float. A negative integer close to zero can lose its entire value during cancellation. → [I2F from a 64-bit integer rounds once](../reference/shaders.md#i2f-from-a-64-bit-integer-rounds-once)
- `F2FP.BF16.PACK_AB` returns a pair of F16 values, and `F2FP.RELU.PACK_AB` keeps negative values. A toward-zero pack can round away from zero or turn a finite value into infinity. → [F2FP packs BF16 and applies RELU and RZ](../reference/shaders.md#f2fp-packs-bf16-and-applies-relu-and-rz)
- an Ampere shader using `dp4a` or `dp2a` fails translation with `Ampere instruction 226 ... is not implemented` for the register form. → [IDP integer dot products](../reference/shaders.md#idp-integer-dot-products)
- an Ampere shader using `sad.u32` fails translation with `Ampere instruction 214 ... is not implemented` for the register form. → [VABSDIFF, the absolute difference with an add](../reference/shaders.md#vabsdiff-the-absolute-difference-with-an-add)
- none in the log: everything drawn with an engine's scene data lands on the first instance's transform, or at nothing. In the MSL, a bounds-checked read has a constant index (`vp_s2._m0[0]`) just after a comparison of the register it should use. → [A guarded load whose destination is its address](../reference/shaders.md#a-guarded-load-whose-destination-is-its-address)
- the scene goes white with a yellow fringe: a few objects hold the largest value their target can, and bloom spreads it over the picture. Clamping a shader's terms one by one finds a value that is a register's stale contents. Or characters lit from an environment cube turn bright white in certain places only, and the cube's mean is far above its value elsewhere. → [A deferred write under a false guard](../reference/shaders.md#a-deferred-write-under-a-false-guard)
- `Ampere instruction f63` (or `f6c`, `364`, `367`, `394`) `is not implemented`, or `SURED form 0310e000`, in the build. → [Ampere forms with a handle in a register, and SURED's other operations](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-forms-with-a-handle-in-a-register-and-sureds-other-operations)
- `Ampere instruction 21b at … is not implemented` (`…0405721b`) or `Ampere instruction 925 at 0x0 is not implemented` (`040e0200000e4000 …7925`) in the build. → [Ampere BMSK and the read of a covered pixel](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-bmsk-and-the-read-of-a-covered-pixel)
- a Switch 2 game's water or another full-screen surface draws black (or as a flat dark colour under the fog) while its normals and depth are right; a texture dump of the target shows the colour channel at 0 where the surface is, and building the shader without fast maths changes nothing. → [The fragment's shading-rate size reads 1 x 1](../reference/shaders.md#the-fragments-shading-rate-size-reads-1-x-1)
- no wrong picture seen so far. In the MSL, a shader with a 0x886 vote has `if (false)` and `false ? a : b` where a uniform predicate guards a value or a branch, and a lane guard on the same number reads `simd_all` or `simd_any` of its own predicate. → [VOTEU writes the uniform predicate](../reference/shaders.md#voteu-writes-the-uniform-predicate)
- `[RED] <key>: the translator deleted N bindless accesses` in the build (Losses a shader build reports; `Failed to find handle source for bindless access` in its reflection's notes), for a shader with TXD through a uniform register (0xf6c). The MSL has no gradient sample, and what it read comes back 0. → [TXD through a uniform register kept no handle](../reference/shaders.md#txd-through-a-uniform-register-kept-no-handle)
- `Failed to find handle source for bindless access` in the build's translator notes, for a read whose handle is two words of the game's own uniform buffer, and what it read comes back 0. Or nothing in the log: the read binds `fp_t_cb3_<word>` or `fp_t_cb2_<word>`, a texture word with no sampler word, so its sampler is `samp_tex`'s guess, where the shader's other reads of that word bind both. → [Handles built under a guard the read's guard cannot be tied to](../reference/shaders.md#handles-built-under-a-guard-the-reads-guard-cannot-be-tied-to)
- in the build's log, `[RED] <key>: the translator deleted N bindless accesses (<type> N): their results read 0` or `[RED] <key>: its library builds only without its integer output, dual source entry points`, and `[RED] lost without failing: …` with the counts per kind (the NSP script repeats that one line). In `regress.py`, `NEW-FAIL: N shaders with more … than in the baseline`. → [Losses a shader build reports](../reference/shaders.md#losses-a-shader-build-reports)
- `Ampere instruction 80d ... (000fc80003fe0000c43211110000780d) is not implemented` for every vertex shader of the game, so nothing draws. → [A guard band cull in every vertex shader (0x80d)](../reference/shaders.md#a-guard-band-cull-in-every-vertex-shader-0x80d)
- `Ampere instruction <op> at 0x… is not implemented`, `F2FP.MERGE_C` or `F2I from a float that is not 32-bit` in the shader build; those shaders are left out and their draws and dispatches skipped. → [Ampere instructions new in Pokémon Pokopia](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-instructions-new-in-pokémon-pokopia)
- an ordered not-equal comparison is true with a NaN operand, so a NaN guard lets it through. An inverted result is wrong too; NVIDIA's compiler can implement unordered equal by inverting ordered not-equal. No game symptom has been identified. → [Ordered not-equal becomes unordered in MSL](../reference/shaders.md#ordered-not-equal-becomes-unordered-in-msl)
- single bright dots at thin far geometry (railings, track edges), changing frame to frame as the camera moves, already in the lit picture before anti-aliasing. Most of them come from another cause (below). → [Comparisons with .FTZ](../reference/shaders.md#comparisons-with-ftz)
- none on the Mac. Suspected as a cause of GPU hangs (`kIOGPUCommandBufferCallbackErrorHang`) in compute, task and mesh shaders that return before a `threadgroup_barrier`. → [Compute threads that return before a barrier](../reference/shaders.md#compute-threads-that-return-before-a-barrier)
- a GPU hang (`kIOGPUCommandBufferCallbackErrorHang`, `[RED] the host has waited 10 s for GPU work`) in a compute shader whose threads poll a flag in a loop with no BAR.SYNC: a persistent work queue. → [MEMBAR is a fence, not a barrier](../reference/shaders.md#membar-is-a-fence-not-a-barrier)
- the game exits but compute work keeps the Mac GPU busy, `kill -9` does not reclaim it, and WindowServer can stop until a reboot. PRAGMATA's reporters needed seven reboots, including after a game abort on main `9ccbd6b1` (issue #2664). Kirby Air Riders' particle submission hung twice in two races; the loop guard's author reports none in sixteen later sessions, whose builds also carried other changes (PR #2254). → [Compute loops that outlive the process](../reference/shaders.md#compute-loops-that-outlive-the-process)
- a GPU hang on the iPhone (`Caused GPU Hang Error`, `kIOGPUCommandBufferCallbackErrorHang`, then `FATAL GPU access revoked`) in a scene that ran before chain d24f05cf9d78 (ccd834e0); the failed command buffer lists a compute shader with shared memory. Shared memory read before the other threads of the group wrote it can also give wrong values without a hang. → [A BAR.SYNC right after a MEMBAR lost](../reference/shaders.md#a-barsync-right-after-a-membar-lost)
- `Ampere instruction f55 at 0x… (000fe20000000000000000001a0f7f55) is not implemented` in the shader build; those shaders are left out. → [Barrier moves around warp shuffles](../reference/shaders.md#barrier-moves-around-warp-shuffles)
- `Ampere instruction 946 at 0x… (000fde00038000000000000000007946) is not implemented` in the shader build; those shaders are left out. → [YIELD](../reference/shaders.md#yield)
- `Ampere b82 at 0x…: LDC with a bindless bank or an address mode (…00008a00…7b82)` in the shader build; those shaders are left out. → [LDC with the bank in its address register (`.IS`)](../reference/shaders.md#ldc-with-the-bank-in-its-address-register-is)
- `reflection conversion failed: B binding fp_c15: 536839244 bytes read at fixed offsets` at load, and the draw is skipped; the shader's MSL reads `fp_c15._m0[33552452]`, a slot the game binds with a few words. → [LDC's 16-bit immediate: signed beside a register, the address alone](../reference/shaders.md#ldcs-16-bit-immediate-signed-beside-a-register-the-address-alone)
- 64-bit arithmetic in a shader gives wild results: the 64-bit division behind an exposure average returned 893011 for 218, so the exposure stayed at the end of its range. With only the pair fixed it still gave 2 for 340 / 1. → [IMAD.HI adds a register pair, and IMAD.X its carry](../reference/shaders.md#imadhi-adds-a-register-pair-and-imadx-its-carry)
- a scene lit by many small lights comes out too dark, with its clustered light lists empty for most clusters. → [NVN2 compute reads its workgroup counts at c0 + 4](../reference/shaders.md#nvn2-compute-reads-its-workgroup-counts-at-c0--4)
- `Ampere instruction 361` (or `f60`, `370`, `38d` and others) refused, or hundreds of `Failed to find handle source for bindless access of type "textureCube"` when the handle was taken from the wrong register: units drawn white, the rest of the scene right. → [Texture handles of SDK 22's Ampere code](../reference/shaders.md#texture-handles-of-sdk-22s-ampere-code)
- `reflection conversion failed: B binding cp_t_cb196610_10280074Smplr has no resolved slot number` at load, then `the dispatched program has no compute shader`; the shader's image has no slot either (`cp_i_tcb_AA_`). → [NVN2 compute's separate handles in c[0]](../reference/shaders.md#nvn2-computes-separate-handles-in-c0)
- the GPU never finishes the first 3D frame: `[RED] the host has waited 10 s for GPU work`, with persistent compute shaders (a loop on a work counter, `threadgroup_barrier` inside) among the failed command buffer's shaders. → [NVN2 compute's second push block holds buffer entries](../reference/shaders.md#nvn2-computes-second-push-block-holds-buffer-entries)
- `Ampere … bank 25` (to 29) refused, or `stage N cbN has no uniform buffer bound` for graphics shaders that read buffers through an address. Then, with those banks taken for the uniform slots: `[RED] graphics push constants into block 5 (0 + 0x10): the host keeps 2, dropped`, `sbufp reg3 dummy` (to reg5) for a mesh shader in the pass dump, and everything a visibility buffer draws comes out black while the rest of the frame is lit. → [NVN2 graphics stages' buffer banks (c[25] to c[29]) are push blocks](../reference/shaders.md#nvn2-graphics-stages-buffer-banks-c25-to-c29-are-push-blocks)
- `Ampere … is not implemented` for ALU forms, ULDC (0xab9) or LDC (0x582) with bit 91 set. → [Uniform words read through a buffer address (cx)](../reference/shaders.md#uniform-words-read-through-a-buffer-address-cx)
- `reflection conversion failed: B binding fp_t_cb2_170 has no resolved slot number` at load (the draw is skipped), the build summary counting the binding under *slot was not decoded*; or, once the read's other inputs are known, `Failed to find handle source for bindless access of type "texture2D"` and the read answers 0. `DMSH_BINDLESS_TRACE=1` on a `dmsh_xlate` job prints what a refused handle is made of. → [A handle pair ORed sampler word first](../reference/shaders.md#a-handle-pair-ored-sampler-word-first)
- menu labels and other text disappear while their backgrounds and icons render. → [A buffer texture with a two-word handle](../reference/shaders.md#a-buffer-texture-with-a-two-word-handle)
- a Switch 2 game's frame rate swings between 60 and 30 fps (Donkey Kong Bananza's own controller, `main+0x204a18`, drops to 30 after two frames of 20 ms from their start to the GPU's end, and comes back after six), and the GPU profile (`DM_NVN_GPUPROF`) shows one compute dispatch of 2-6.5 ms a frame: agl's upscaler network (`agl_ndsr.pck`, shader `7629d857a0a93833`). → [Ampere HMMA through simdgroup_matrix](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-hmma-through-simdgroup_matrix)
- a Switch 2 game's movie or upscaler network runs slow on the Mac and iPhone, and the GPU profile (`DM_NVN_GPUPROF` with each dispatch in an encoder of its own, then `DM_NVN_DISPATCH_OWN=1`, since removed) shows its compute dispatches, small grids of one to four SIMD-groups a threadgroup that loop over tiles, at 1-3 ms each; the dispatches' shaders call `dmsh_hmma16816_f16` hundreds of times (Ampere HMMA through simdgroup_matrix). → [HMMA chains keep their matrices in the SIMD-group](../reference/shaders.md#hmma-chains-keep-their-matrices-in-the-simd-group)
- a Switch 2 game's movie or upscaler network output has dotted streaks, every other pixel of a few rows wrong, in other places each run, while its input is clean. Through agl's NDSR upscaler a few such pixels a frame grow into white blotches over the whole picture: Ocarina of Time's Kokiri Forest on an M4 Pro on chain 844bf1a5ea8f, with the game's own upscaler (the default); with `DM_UPSCALER=metalfx` in its place, no blotches. → [LDSM reads rows other lanes stored](../reference/shaders.md#ldsm-reads-rows-other-lanes-stored)
- dotted horizontal rows across the picture at a regular spacing, in fog and light, with the game's upscaler and with `DM_UPSCALER=metalfx` alike (MetalFX then replaced only `agl_ndsr.pck`'s shaders): Ocarina of Time in Kokiri Forest, Hyrule Field and the castle town since chain 23a1ea1db092. The rows are the tiles of agl's `agl_ndsr_vr.pck` / `agl_ndsr_vr_light.pck` networks (about 28.8 pixels of the 1536-wide input). → [An HMMA outside a chain overwrote the chain's accumulator](../reference/shaders.md#an-hmma-outside-a-chain-overwrote-the-chains-accumulator)
- a Switch 2 game's movie or upscaler network gives values a few f16 steps off the console's, in about half the outputs of each HMMA. Nothing looks broken; it is the network's arithmetic, compounded layer by layer. → [HMMA rounds its f16 accumulator once per 8 products](../reference/shaders.md#hmma-rounds-its-f16-accumulator-once-per-8-products)
- a warp shuffle whose lane comes from a register with bits above bit 4 set returns the thread's own value instead of the requested lane's. Orin probes found 207 wrong IDX results, 196 BFLY results and 179 segmented IDX results, each out of 1,024 rows (PR #2044). → [SHFL takes its lane from the low 5 bits](../reference/shaders.md#shfl-takes-its-lane-from-the-low-5-bits)
- none that shows on its own: a translated instruction gives other bits than the console's GPU for some inputs (a NaN operand, a lane index past 31, a directed rounding), and the picture is off only where a shader depends on them. The translator tests check the MSL a few encodings give, not the values the shader computes. → [Ampere instructions checked against an Ampere GPU](../reference/shaders.md#ampere-instructions-checked-against-an-ampere-gpu)
- FADD, FMUL and FFMA with `.RM`, `.RP` or `.RZ` can return the round-to-nearest result, one ULP away from the result requested by the shader. → [Directed rounding in FADD, FMUL and FFMA](../reference/shaders.md#directed-rounding-in-fadd-fmul-and-ffma)
- minimum or maximum returns the wrong sign for a zero result. On the recorded Orin inputs, `max(+0, -0)` returns +0 while the translation can return -0. A subsequent reciprocal or sign test can distinguish the results. → [FMNMX and HMNMX2 signed-zero ordering](../reference/shaders.md#fmnmx-and-hmnmx2-signed-zero-ordering)
- HADD2.FTZ, HMUL2.FTZ and HFMA2.FTZ keep half subnormals instead of flushing them. A subnormal times infinity consequently gives infinity instead of NaN. → [Half-pair .FTZ flushes f16 subnormals](../reference/shaders.md#half-pair-ftz-flushes-f16-subnormals)
- `NotSupportedException: Ampere instruction 235` or `435` prevents a shader from translating. Half `.RELU` arithmetic also turns NaN into zero instead of retaining NaN. → [HFMA2.MMA, the half-pair FMA on the tensor pipe](../reference/shaders.md#hfma2mma-the-half-pair-fma-on-the-tensor-pipe)
- a packed HFMA2 result is one half ULP off near a rounding midpoint. For example, `-3136 × -8.125 + 0.000124693` produces 25488 on Orin and 25472 with the old translation. → [HFMA2 single rounding to half](../reference/shaders.md#hfma2-single-rounding-to-half)
- a Switch 2 game's movies and the title behind them run below 60 fps on a Mac with a smaller GPU, and the GPU profile (`DM_NVN_GPUPROF`) shows almost all of the frame in a dozen compute dispatches of `nn::ndve`'s movie network: Ocarina of Time's title at about 24 fps and 38 ms of GPU a frame on an M1 Pro with 16 GPU cores, 24.8 ms on an M1 Max (issue 1375), 18.9 ms of compute on an M4 with 8 (HMMA chains keep their matrices in the SIMD-group); 11-14 ms of the 16.7 on an M6, an M4 Pro or an M5 Max. → [The NDVE movie network by the host](../reference/shaders.md#the-ndve-movie-network-by-the-host)
- on an M1 or M2, the game's own upscaler takes about 5 ms of GPU a frame at docked resolution, most of it one compute dispatch: agl's NDSR network `fa3033d05c0a41d6` (`agl_ndsr_vr_light.pck`, 1920x1080 to 2560x1440). Ocarina of Time's Kakariko Village ran at 31-34.5 fps on an M2 Pro, 39 with that dispatch dropped (`DM_NVN_SKIP_CS`); its compute occupancy was 14 % (Metal System Trace, GPU counters). → [agl's NDSR network on simdgroup matrices, in two passes](../reference/shaders.md#agls-ndsr-network-on-simdgroup-matrices-in-two-passes)
- a development build (`--platform mac`, no offline pipeline bundle) never shows the title movie: the run stops at the movie's first frame with the submit thread waiting in `newComputePipelineStateWithFunction`, and `sample` shows `MTLCompilerService` in LLVM's `InstructionCombiningPass` for minutes on end. A packed app does not show it: its bundle holds the pipelines. → [The game's movie kernels compiled although the host's engine runs them](../reference/shaders.md#the-games-movie-kernels-compiled-although-the-hosts-engine-runs-them)
- a vertex or compute shader reads zeros from NVN's driver segment `c0`. → [Driver-segment reads outside fragment shaders](../reference/shaders.md#driver-segment-reads-outside-fragment-shaders)
- flickering hard-edged white tiles (tiled lighting with every tile row getting row 0's or other rows' lights). → [Compute `c0`: dispatch size and uniform buffer table](../reference/shaders.md#compute-c0-dispatch-size-and-uniform-buffer-table)
- a storage buffer the shader stores to or uses atomically has `rw: false` in its generated reflection; replay does not record its GPU writes for dependent buffer reads. → [Storage-buffer write flag in translation metadata](../reference/shaders.md#storage-buffer-write-flag-in-translation-metadata)
- point sprites of size 0 (Odyssey's Cap Kingdom fog never parted around Mario). → [Graphics `c0 + 0x610`: the largest point size](../reference/shaders.md#graphics-c0--0x610-the-largest-point-size)
- occlusion queries that always return 0 samples (Z-A: characters' legs folded back from the hips once they walked); every instanced draw's transform taken for instance 0. → [Graphics `c0 + 0x644`: the draw's base instance](../reference/shaders.md#graphics-c0--0x644-the-draws-base-instance)
- flat grey or background-coloured triangles with straight edges across part of the scene, changing shape from frame to frame while the camera stands still; dropping the colour pass's fragment shaders in groups (`DM_NVN_SKIP_FS`) did not remove them, as the instances are misplaced in the depth pre-pass too, whose depth the colour pass tests for equality. → [The base instance of indirect draws comes from Metal](../reference/shaders.md#the-base-instance-of-indirect-draws-comes-from-metal)
- `[RED] shader … reflection conversion failed: reads driver-bank locations at run time that the replay layer cannot fill: c0 dynamic index c0+0x630` (or `c0 dynamic index` alone), and that shader's draws are missing (`[RED] shader … (pixel) failed to load`); the build notes `Shader instruction Pixld is not implemented` for pixel shaders; `[RED] NVN unimplemented function called: nvnProgramSetSampleShading`. → [Pixel `c0 + 0x620`: the sample index and GLSLC's sample position table](../reference/shaders.md#pixel-c0--0x620-the-sample-index-and-glslcs-sample-position-table)
- a menu's labels drawn as plain bars, other menus empty except for the cursor (one pool's textures overwritten by another's); a black screen when IDs past a fixed table read as empty slots. → [Texture and sampler pools with overlapping IDs](../reference/shaders.md#texture-and-sampler-pools-with-overlapping-ids)
- red lines from the list of command buffers that bound a non-first pool (6,734 in one DARK SOULS: REMASTERED run). → [Bases of bound handles kept in the command buffer](../reference/shaders.md#bases-of-bound-handles-kept-in-the-command-buffer)
- terrain is magenta, trees are white and colour conversion reads uninitialized data; the compute pass dump shows `W ... tid=360 dummy` despite a registered writable 3D texture. → [Descriptor handles use the execution pools](../reference/shaders.md#descriptor-handles-use-the-execution-pools)
- distant walls and floors smeared sideways (DARK SOULS: REMASTERED): tiling textures took a clamp-to-edge sampler and repeated their edge column. → [Handles from uniform buffers take the bound pools' bases](../reference/shaders.md#handles-from-uniform-buffers-take-the-bound-pools-bases)
- the 3D scene black, only particles and the HUD drawn; pass dump `B` lines all `tid=0 dummy`. → [Deferred texture binds read at execution](../reference/shaders.md#deferred-texture-binds-read-at-execution)
- bindless handles read from a uniform buffer name the wrong texture when the game writes them earlier in the same submission. → [Handles in uniform buffers written earlier in the same submission](../reference/shaders.md#handles-in-uniform-buffers-written-earlier-in-the-same-submission)
- a storage buffer reported with `cb_slot` 3, which had no NVN slot. → [Storage buffer addresses in the game's own uniform buffer](../reference/shaders.md#storage-buffer-addresses-in-the-games-own-uniform-buffer)
- whatever a shader writes through a pointer it read from memory never happens. The build summary counted `Failed to find storage buffer for global memory operation "Store"`, and the shader's MSL had a store function with an empty body. In Donkey Kong Bananza the tunnel's walls and floor were black and only the loose rocks drew. → [Global memory at an address loaded from memory](../reference/shaders.md#global-memory-at-an-address-loaded-from-memory)
- GPU-driven geometry never draws: its indirect draw records keep count 0 and its visibility buffer stays clear, although the culling compute runs every frame. A persistent culling kernel takes its work items (its work counter passes the item count) and writes nothing. Its build notes do not say `traced to no storage buffer`: the loads are traced, to a storage buffer the address is in on only some paths. In PRAGMATA the title's Earth and Moon and the intro's ship and cockpit were black. → [Global memory at a storage buffer's address on some paths only](../reference/shaders.md#global-memory-at-a-storage-buffers-address-on-some-paths-only)
- an NVN2 shader that reads a constant buffer through a bindless handle (`cx[UR][offset]`) gets a dummy buffer or zeros, with `[RED] shader <key>: the buffer address the game pushed at c[24] + 0x… is 0x1800830776a28, in no memory pool: bound to a dummy buffer` (an "address" of 49 bits, with a small number in its top bits). → [Bindless constant buffer handles in the hardware's layout](../reference/shaders.md#bindless-constant-buffer-handles-in-the-hardwares-layout)
- skinned meshes lose parts or explode: players without heads in the lineup cutscene, and with the uniform test rewrite on, giant triangles over the pitch at pauses and in cutscenes. No red line; the shaders build. In a shader's Metal source, a storage buffer read's word index is `c[x][y] >> 2` where the previous chain had `(c[x][y] << 4) >> 2`. → [A uniform word << 4 taken for a bindless handle where it is the offset](../reference/shaders.md#a-uniform-word--4-taken-for-a-bindless-handle-where-it-is-the-offset)
- a raw load reads 0 when its pointer names a pool omitted from the raw table. Ocarina of Time's eight culling task shaders then compute `(min(end - start, 256) - 1) >> 5` from two zero range ends: 134,217,727 iterations and an isolated GPU hang. → [Raw global memory through a pointer read when the draw is bound](../reference/shaders.md#raw-global-memory-through-a-pointer-read-when-the-draw-is-bound)
- a pass whose shaders read memory through loaded addresses takes several milliseconds on the GPU for little work. `[gpuprof]` names the pass, and its shaders' build notes say `Global memory operation "Load" traced to no storage buffer`. Ocarina of Time's foliage mesh shader `73dc0b90c4082e43` (`Foliage.Product.110`, 135 such reads) took 10.9 ms of a 683x384 frame; 2,241 of the game's shaders (vertex, compute, fragment and mesh) have such a lookup, some hundreds of them. → [Raw global memory lookups that start from their last entry](../reference/shaders.md#raw-global-memory-lookups-that-start-from-their-last-entry)
- a pipeline whose shader reads memory through loaded addresses takes seconds to compile on a cold Metal cache, and the GPU waits at its first draw (Native mesh pipelines compiled at their first draw); its library is large for its MSL. Ocarina of Time's foliage mesh shader `3439aaa071d868ae` makes 141 raw accesses; its pipeline took 1.9 s on an M2 Air, and the route's 54 task / mesh pipelines 25 s at the first start. → [Raw global lookups built once for every call](../reference/shaders.md#raw-global-lookups-built-once-for-every-call)
- on an M2 Pro the picture flickers once, or freezes for a moment, and after a few of those the game quits (`launcher: the host exited with 70`). The log has `Caused GPU Hang Error (00000003:kIOGPUCommandBufferCallbackErrorHang)` on a command buffer whose shader list (Naming the shaders of a GPU fault) holds task and mesh shaders, then `Ignored (for causing prior/excessive GPU errors)` on every submission (A GPU whose access macOS revoked stops the game). macOS writes one `/Library/Logs/DiagnosticReports/gpuEvent-dmhost-*.ips` per restart, with `"restart_reason_desc":"firmware-detected lockup"` and `fw_ta_state` busy (the geometry stage: vertex, task and mesh functions). It comes at some camera cuts and area loads and at moments with no pattern; standing or walking in the same place does not show it. → [A noinline call in a task or mesh function locks up an M2's GPU](../reference/shaders.md#a-noinline-call-in-a-task-or-mesh-function-locks-up-an-m2s-gpu)
- Ocarina of Time logs `task / mesh pipeline prewarm failed` with `XPC_ERROR_CONNECTION_INTERRUPTED`; the compiler backend dies with SIGBUS in `llvm::MachineOperand::setReg` while compiling a mesh program. Other compiles interrupted by the same service death can fail too. The isolated pipeline can compile on an M4 Pro even when an offline compile for an older GPU fails. → [A noinline raw lookup crashes the M1 and M2 mesh compiler](../reference/shaders.md#a-noinline-raw-lookup-crashes-the-m1-and-m2-mesh-compiler)
- on an M2 Pro (`applegpu_g14s`), `[RED] task / mesh pipeline prewarm failed: task a7f22b4a795ff4d1_b mesh ac2f3feb11427426_b fragment eddc12907cefc9af_b: Compilation failed due to an interrupted connection: XPC_ERROR_CONNECTION_INTERRUPTED` at every start, with `[RED] pipeline archive: the compiler service died under a compile`; `MTLCompilerService` dies with SIGBUS in `llvm::MachineOperand::setReg` under `AGCModule<AGX::Impl::MeshProgramKey>::compileLLVMObject`. The archive period the crash falls in is not written, so its pipelines are captured again at every start, other compiles under way then fail with the same error, and objects the pipeline draws stay white. This is the crash that remained on that Mac with the raw search inline (A noinline raw lookup crashes the M1 and M2 mesh compiler). → [A shuffle of a constant crashes Apple's M2 mesh compiler](../reference/shaders.md#a-shuffle-of-a-constant-crashes-apples-m2-mesh-compiler)
- a pass of mesh, vertex or compute shaders whose build notes say `Global memory operation "Load" traced to no storage buffer` takes milliseconds of its stage's time for little drawing, at any resolution.
